$(document).ready(function(e) {
	//START SCRIPT FOR MEGA MENU
	$(".sm_megamenu_menu > li:nth-of-type(1n+11)").css({"display":"none"});
	$(".more-view").click(function(e) {
        $(".sm_megamenu_menu > li:nth-of-type(1n+11)").slideToggle();
		$(".sm_megamenu_menu > li:nth-of-type(1n+11)").css({"overflow":"visible" });
    });
	//START SCRIPT FOR MEGA MENU
	
	//START SCRIPT FOR COMMAN JS FOR ALL PAGE
	$(".publication_content1 > a").addClass("publication_title");
	$(".checkout-container").addClass("container");
	$(".footer-top").addClass("container");
	$(".footer-links-w").addClass("container");
	$(".full-wrapper>div").removeClass("container");
	$(".footer-wrapper>div").removeClass("container");
	$('.tab_container').hide();
	$('.tab_container:first').show();
	$('.loading-mask').css("display", "none");
	$('ul.tab li:first').addClass('active');
	$('ul.tab li a').click(function(){
		$('ul.tab li').removeClass('active');
		$(this).parent().addClass('active');
		var currentTab = $(this).attr('href');
		$('.tab_container').hide();
		$(currentTab).show();
		return false;
	});
	//END SCRIPT FOR COMMAN JS FOR ALL PAGE
		
	//START SCRIPT FOR TOOL TIP ON REGESTRATION PAGE
	$(".msg-display").mouseenter(function(e){
		e.preventdefault
		$(this).next('.imp-msg, .imp-msg1').show();
	});
	$(".msg-display").mouseleave(function(e){
		e.preventdefault
		$(this).next('.imp-msg, .imp-msg1').hide();
	});
    //END SCRIPT FOR TOOL TIP ON REGESTRATION PAGE
	
	//START SCRIPT FOR COMPARE PRODUCT
	$('.tocompare').click(function(){
		$('.block-compare').css('display', 'block', '!important');
	});
	//END SCRIPT FOR COMPARE PRODUCT	
	
	 //START SCRIPT FOR SCROLL TO TOP	
	 $('#yt-totop').click(function() {
		 $('html, body').animate({
			 scrollTop: 0
		 }, 800);
		 return false;
	 });
    //END SCRIPT FOR SCROLL TO TOP	
 
});
</script>

<h1>Welcome to Ken Research</h1>
<p>Ken Research is a Global aggregator and publisher of Market intelligence, equity and economy reports. We provides business intelligence and operational advisory in 300+ verticals underscoring disruptive technologies, emerging business models with precedent analysis and success case studies. Serving 70% of fortune 500 companies globally, some of top consulting companies and Market leaders seek our intelligence to identify new revenue streams, customer/ vendor paradigm and pain points and due diligence on competition.</p>

<div class="serve_client_data">
<ul>
<li>Our researchers seek opinion and facts from 25,000+ research journals, annual reports, news articles, white papers, conference presentation, government reports and custom databank.</li>
<li>We access 150+ paid data sources to get accurate and authentic statistics. Over 65+ publishers contribute business intelligence on our panel.</li>
<li>We use Sensitivity, Scenario analysis, Multi-Factor Regression Analysis backed by industry leaders’ opinion polls to provide the future forecast</li>
<li>We don’t just provide Research but advice on how to Operationalize Business Model by connecting to Right Vendors, KOLs.</li>
</ul>

<script>


function submitPostReg(){
	var form       = document.RegPost;
	var dataString = $(form).serialize();
	
	$.ajax({
		type:'POST',
		url:'https://www.kenresearch.com/AjexPaidPostRegistration.php',
		data: dataString,
		
		success: function(data){
			html= data.trim();
			if(html=='Successfully'){
				$("#hidePostsForm").css('display', 'none', 'important');
				$("#PostFormMsg").css('display', 'inline', 'important');	
				window.setTimeout(function(){location.reload()},5000)
			}else{
				$('#PostsResult').html(data);
				$('#hidden_value').val(data);
			}
		}
	});
	return false;
}



<!--AJAX SCRIPT FOR Company Brochure START-->
function DownloadBroc() {
	var form = document.dl_brochure;
	var dataString = $(form).serialize();
	$.ajax({
		type:'POST',
		url:'https://www.kenresearch.com/download_brochure_ajax.php',
        data: dataString,
	    success: function(data){
			html= data.trim();
			if(html=='Successfully'){
				$("#dow-brochure").css('display', 'none', 'important');
				$("#DownloadFormMsg").css('display', 'inline', 'important');
				window.location="https://www.kenresearch.com/download.php?link=broccer/Ken-Research-Brochure.pdf";
				window.setTimeout(function(){location.reload()},5000)
			}else{
				$('#BrochurResult').html(data);
				$('#hidden_value').val(data);
			}
		}
	});
return false;}
<!--AJAX SCRIPT FOR COMPANY BROCHURE END-->


<!--START AJAX SCRIPT FOR APPLY FOR JOB-->
$(document).ready(function (e) {
	$("#ApplyJob").on('submit',(function(e) {
		e.preventDefault();
		$.ajax({
			url: "https://www.kenresearch.com/carrier_ajax.php",
			type: "POST",
			data:  new FormData(this),
			contentType: false,
    	    cache: false,
			processData:false,
			success: function(data){
				html= data.trim();
				
				if(html=='Successfully'){
					$("#hidejobapp").css('display', 'none', 'important');
					$("#jobmsgshow").css('display', 'block', 'important');
					window.setTimeout(function(){location.reload()},2000)
				}else {
					$("#carrer_error").html(data);
				    $('.no-click').hide();
				}
			},
		});
	}));
});
<!--END AJAX SCRIPT FOR APPLY FOR JOB-->


<!--START AJAX SCRIPT FOR ENGAGE WITH US-->
function engagewith() {
var form = document.engagewithus;
var dataString = $(form).serialize();
$.ajax({
	type:'POST',
    url:'https://www.kenresearch.com/engage_with_ajax.php',
    data: dataString,
    success: function(data){
		html= data.trim();
		if(html=='Successfully'){
			$("#engage-us").css('display', 'none', 'important');
			$("#EngagewithusMsg").css('display', 'block', 'important');
			window.setTimeout(function(){location.reload()},4000)
		}else{
			$('#EngageResult').html(data);
			$('#hidden_value').val(data);
		}
	}
});
return false;
}
<!--END AJAX SCRIPT FOR ENGAGE WITH US -->


$(document).ready(function(e) {
	
	<!--START SCRIPT FOR FORGET PASS POPUP-->
	$("#forgotpassclick").click(function(){
		$(".alllogindata").hide();
	    $(".allforgotpasss").show();
	    $(".wrong_pwd").hide();
	})
	$(".backTologin").click(function(){
		$(".alllogindata").show();
		$(".allforgotpasss").hide();
		$(".wrong_pwd").hide();
	})
	<!--START SCRIPT FOR FORGET PASS POPUP-->
	
	<!--START AJAX SCRIPT FOR EMPLOYE LOGIN-->
	$('#emplogin').click(function(){
		var EmpEmail = $('#Emp_email').val();
		var EmpPass = $('#Emp_pass').val();
		
		$.ajax({
			url: 'https://www.kenresearch.com/employe_login_ajax.php',
			type: 'POST',
			data : {
				EmpLogEmail           : EmpEmail,
				EmpLogPass            : EmpPass,
			},
			success: function(data) {
				$('#Emp-login').html(data);
				$('#hidden_value').val(data);
			},
			
			error: function (xhr, status, error) {
				alert(status);
				alert(xhr.responseText);
			}
		});
	});
	<!--END AJAX SCRIPT FOR EMPLOYE LOGIN-->
	
});

 $(".deleteCart").click(function(){
	 $("#loading").show(400).fadeIn();
	 var datas=$(this).attr('p');
	 //alert("Deatale cart value: "+datas);
	 var dataString='types=DetateCart'+'&text='+datas;
	 $.ajax({
		 type:'POST',
		 url:'https://www.kenresearch.com/addtocartdate.php',
		 data: dataString,
		 success: function(data){
			 html= data.trim();
			 var res = data.split("-");
			 $("#cartcounts").html(res[1]);
			 //alert(data);
			 if(res[0]==1){
				 //loadMyCart();
				 //$('.'+datas+'_tables').hide(400).fadeOut();
				window.setTimeout(function(){location.reload()},500)
			 }
			 //$("#cartcounts").html(data);
		 }
	 });
		return false;
 })

 }
